===== 6. 手机子菜单自动生成函数 =====

  data\navigation.ts:1444:/* ================================
  data\navigation.ts:1445:   根据 Mega 分类生成手机端二级导航
  data\navigation.ts:1446:
  data\navigation.ts:1447:   说明：
  data\navigation.ts:1448:   1. PC 端产品中心使用 productMegaDropdown.categories
  data\navigation.ts:1449:   2. 手机端产品中心使用 mobileChildren
  data\navigation.ts:1450:   3. 为了避免 PC 和手机端分类重复维护，这里自动从 Mega 分类生成手机端菜单
  data\navigation.ts:1451:   4. 后续只要修改 productMegaDropdown.categories，手机端产品菜单会自动同步
  data\navigation.ts:1452:================================ */
  data\navigation.ts:1453:
> data\navigation.ts:1454:function createMobileChildrenFromMegaCategories(
  data\navigation.ts:1455:  megaDropdown: MegaDropdown,
  data\navigation.ts:1456:  anchor: string,
  data\navigation.ts:1457:  keyPrefix: string
  data\navigation.ts:1458:): MobileNavChild[] {
  data\navigation.ts:1459:  return megaDropdown.categories
  data\navigation.ts:1460:    .filter((category) => category.enabled)
  data\navigation.ts:1461:    .sort((a, b) => a.order - b.order)
  data\navigation.ts:1462:    .map((category) => ({
  data\navigation.ts:1463:      key: `${keyPrefix}-${category.key}`,
  data\navigation.ts:1464:      label: category.title,
  data\navigation.ts:1465:      href: anchorPath(anchor),
  data\navigation.ts:1466:      order: category.order,
  data\navigation.ts:1467:      enabled: category.enabled,
  data\navigation.ts:1468:    }));
  data\navigation.ts:1469:}
  data\navigation.ts:1470:
  data\navigation.ts:1471:/* ================================
  data\navigation.ts:1472:   官网一级导航数据
  data\navigation.ts:1473:================================ */
  data\navigation.ts:1474:
  data\navigation.ts:1475:/* ================================
  data\navigation.ts:1476:   联系与合作下拉栏数据
  data\navigation.ts:1477:
  data\navigation.ts:1478:   说明：
  data\navigation.ts:1479:   1. 中文站不显示这个下拉栏，只显示「联系我们」
  data\navigation.ts:1480:   2. 外语站显示「Contact & Partnership」下拉栏
  data\navigation.ts:1481:   3. 下拉栏包含：
  data\navigation.ts:1482:      - Contact Us
  data\navigation.ts:1483:      - Become a Distributor
  data\navigation.ts:1484:================================ */
  data\navigation.ts:1485:
